      <span id="qty-display" class="w-8 text-center text-sm font-bold">1</span>
      <button onclick="changeQty(1)" class="w-9 h-10 flex items-center justify-center text-muted">+</button>
    </div>
    <!-- Add to cart -->
    <button onclick="addToCart({{ product.pk }})"
            class="flex-1 h-11 bg-primary text-white font-bold rounded-2xl active:scale-95 transition-all text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
      <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M3 3h2l.4 2M7 13h10l4-8H5.4M7 13L5.4 5M7 13l-2 9m12-9l2 9M9 21a1 1 0 1 0 0-2 1 1 0 0 0 0 2zm6 0a1 1 0 1 0 0-2 1 1 0 0 0 0 2z"/>
      </svg>

      Out of Stock

  <!-- Toast -->
  <div id="toast" class="fixed bottom-24 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-dark text-white text-sm font-medium shadow-xl max-w-xs text-center pointer-events-none"></div>

  <script>
    // CSRF
    function getCookie(name) {
      let v = document.cookie.match('(^|;) ?' + name + '=([^;]*)(;|$)');
      return v ? v[2] : null;
    }
    const CSRF = getCookie('csrftoken');

    // Toast
    function showToast(msg, type) {
      const t = document.getElementById('toast');
      t.textContent = msg;
      t.className = `fixed bottom-24 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl text-white text-sm font-medium shadow-xl max-w-xs text-center pointer-events-none show ${type === 'error' ? 'bg-red-500' : 'bg-dark'}`;
      setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 3000);
    }

    // Qty
    let qty = 1;
    const maxQty = {{ product.stock_quantity|default:99 }};
    function changeQty(delta) {
      qty = Math.max(1, Math.min(maxQty, qty + delta));
      document.querySelectorAll('#qty-display, #qty-display-desk').forEach(el => el.textContent = qty);
    }

    // Thumbnail
    function setImg(url) {
      document.getElementById('main-img').src = url;
      document.querySelectorAll('.thumb-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('border-primary'));
    }

    // Add to cart
    function addToCart(productId) {
      fetch('/cart/add/', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': CSRF },
        body: JSON.stringify({ product_id: productId, quantity: qty })
      })
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => {
        if (data.success) {
          showToast(data.message, 'success');
          // Update cart badge
          const badge = document.getElementById('cart-badge');
          if (badge && data.cart_total_items > 0) {
            badge.textContent = data.cart_total_items;
            badge.classList.remove('hidden');
            badge.classList.add('flex');
          }
        } else {
          showToast(data.message, 'error');
        }
      })
      .catch(() => showToast('Something went wrong.', 'error'));
    }
  </script>

</body>
</html>

{# Reusable Pagination Component
Usage in templates (example): include this file with a `page_obj` variable

Requires Django Paginator page_obj context variable
Shows: Previous, page numbers, Next
Mobile-friendly with simplified view on small screens
#}

{% if page_obj.has_other_pages %}
<nav class="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-gray-200 px-4 sm:px-0 mt-8 pt-4">
    
    <!-- Mobile View: Simple Previous/Next -->
    <div class="flex w-0 flex-1 sm:hidden">
        {% if page_obj.has_previous %}
        <a href="?page={{ page_obj.previous_page_number }}" 
           class="inline-flex items-center pr-4 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:text-primary">
            <svg class="mr-2 h-5 w-5 text-gray-400" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
                <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M7.707 14.707a1 1 0 01-1.414 0l-4-4a1 1 0 010-1.414l4-4a1 1 0 011.414 1.414L5.414 9H17a1 1 0 110 2H5.414l2.293 2.293a1 1 0 010 1.414z" clip-rule="evenodd"/>
            </svg>
            Previous
        </a>
        {% endif %}
    </div>
    
    <!-- Mobile: Page Info -->
    <div class="flex sm:hidden">
        <p class="text-sm text-gray-700">
            Page <span class="font-semibold">{{ page_obj.number }}</span> of 
            <span class="font-semibold">{{ page_obj.paginator.num_pages }}</span>
        </p>
    </div>
    
    <!-- Mobile: Next -->
    <div class="flex w-0 flex-1 justify-end sm:hidden">
        {% if page_obj.has_next %}
        <a href="?page={{ page_obj.next_page_number }}" 
           class="inline-flex items-center pl-4 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:text-primary">
            Next
            <svg class="ml-2 h-5 w-5 text-gray-400" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
                <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M12.293 5.293a1 1 0 011.414 0l4 4a1 1 0 010 1.414l-4 4a1 1 0 01-1.414-1.414L14.586 11H3a1 1 0 110-2h11.586l-2.293-2.293a1 1 0 010-1.414z" clip-rule="evenodd"/>
            </svg>
        </a>
        {% endif %}
    </div>
    
    <!-- Desktop View: Full Pagination -->
    <div class="hidden sm:flex sm:flex-1 sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div>
            <p class="text-sm text-gray-700">
                Showing 
                <span class="font-semibold">{{ page_obj.start_index }}</span>
                to 
                <span class="font-semibold">{{ page_obj.end_index }}</span>
                of 
                <span class="font-semibold">{{ page_obj.paginator.count }}</span>
                results
            </p>
        </div>
        
        <div>
            <nav class="isolate inline-flex -space-x-px rounded-lg shadow-sm" aria-label="Pagination">
                
                <!-- Previous Button -->
                {% if page_obj.has_previous %}
                <a href="?page={{ page_obj.previous_page_number }}" 
                   class="relative inline-flex items-center rounded-l-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white border border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50 hover:text-primary transition-colors">
                    <svg class="h-5 w-5" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
                        <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M12.707 5.293a1 1 0 010 1.414L9.414 10l3.293 3.293a1 1 0 01-1.414 1.414l-4-4a1 1 0 010-1.414l4-4a1 1 0 011.414 0z" clip-rule="evenodd"/>
                    </svg>
                    <span class="ml-1">Previous</span>
                </a>
                {% else %}
                <span class="relative inline-flex items-center rounded-l-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 bg-gray-100 border border-gray-300 cursor-not-allowed">
                    <svg class="h-5 w-5" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
                        <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M12.707 5.293a1 1 0 010 1.414L9.414 10l3.293 3.293a1 1 0 01-1.414 1.414l-4-4a1 1 0 010-1.414l4-4a1 1 0 011.414 0z" clip-rule="evenodd"/>
                    </svg>
                    <span class="ml-1">Previous</span>
                </span>
                {% endif %}
                
                <!-- Page Numbers -->
                {% for num in page_obj.paginator.page_range %}
                    {% if page_obj.number == num %}
                    <!-- Current Page -->
                    <span class="relative z-10 inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-primary border border-primary focus:z-20">
                        {{ num }}
                    </span>
                    {% elif num > page_obj.number|add:'-3' and num < page_obj.number|add:'3' %}
                    <!-- Nearby Pages -->
                    <a href="?page={{ num }}" 
                       class="relative inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white border border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50 hover:text-primary transition-colors">
                        {{ num }}
                    </a>
                    {% elif num == 1 or num == page_obj.paginator.num_pages %}
                    <!-- First and Last Page Always Shown -->
                    <a href="?page={{ num }}" 
                       class="relative inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white border border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50 hover:text-primary transition-colors">
                        {{ num }}
                    </a>
                    {% elif num == page_obj.number|add:'-3' or num == page_obj.number|add:'3' %}
                    <!-- Ellipsis -->
                    <span class="relative inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white border border-gray-300">
                        ...
                    </span>
                    {% endif %}
                {% endfor %}
                
                <!-- Next Button -->
                {% if page_obj.has_next %}
                <a href="?page={{ page_obj.next_page_number }}" 
                   class="relative inline-flex items-center rounded-r-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 bg-white border border-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-50 hover:text-primary transition-colors">
                    <span class="mr-1">Next</span>

                    </svg>

                    <span class="mr-1">Next</span>

<!-- Reusable Progress Bar Component
Usage in templates:
{% include 'vendors/components/progress_bar.html' with 
   percentage=75 
   color="purple"
   label="Profile Completion"
   show_label=True
   size="md"
%}

Sizes: sm, md, lg
Colors: purple, blue, green, pink, yellow, red
-->

{% if show_label %}
<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
    <span class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">{{ label|default:"Progress" }}</span>
    <span class="text-sm font-semibold {% if color == 'purple' %}text-primary{% elif color == 'blue' %}text-soft-blue{% elif color == 'green' %}text-green-600{% elif color == 'pink' %}text-pink-accent{% elif color == 'yellow' %}text-yellow-600{% elif color == 'red' %}text-red-600{% else %}text-gray-600{% endif %}">
        {{ percentage|default:0 }}%
    </span>
</div>
{% endif %}

<div class="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden 
            {% if size == 'sm' %}h-2
            {% elif size == 'lg' %}h-4
            {% else %}h-3{% endif %}">
    <div class="h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500 ease-out
                {% if color == 'purple' %}bg-gradient-to-r from-primary to-light-purple
                {% elif color == 'blue' %}bg-gradient-to-r from-soft-blue to-blue-400
                {% elif color == 'green' %}bg-gradient-to-r from-green-500 to-green-400
                {% elif color == 'pink' %}bg-gradient-to-r from-pink-accent to-pink-400
                {% elif color == 'yellow' %}bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-500 to-yellow-400
                {% elif color == 'red' %}bg-gradient-to-r from-red-500 to-red-400
                {% else %}bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-500 to-gray-400{% endif %}"
         style="width: {{ percentage|default:0 }}%"
         x-data="{ width: 0 }"
         x-init="setTimeout(() => width = {{ percentage|default:0 }}, 100)"
         :style="`width: ${width}%`">
    </div>
</div>

{% if show_steps %}
<!-- Step indicators below progress bar -->
<div class="flex justify-between mt-2">
    {% for step in steps %}
    <div class="text-center flex-1">
        <div class="w-8 h-8 mx-auto rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-semibold
                    {% if forloop.counter0 < percentage %}
                        {% if color == 'purple' %}bg-primary text-white
                        {% elif color == 'blue' %}bg-soft-blue text-white
                        {% elif color == 'green' %}bg-green-500 text-white
                        {% elif color == 'pink' %}bg-pink-accent text-white
                        {% elif color == 'yellow' %}bg-yellow-500 text-white
                        {% elif color == 'red' %}bg-red-500 text-white
                        {% else %}bg-gray-500 text-white{% endif %}
                    {% else %}
                        bg-gray-200 text-gray-500
                    {% endif %}">
            {{ forloop.counter }}
        </div>
        <p class="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">{{ step }}</p>
    </div>
    {% endfor %}
</div>
{% endif %}

<!-- Reusable Verification Badge Component
Usage in templates:
{% include 'vendors/components/verification_badge.html' with 
   status="completed"
   label="Verified"
   verification_type="nin"
   size="md"
   show_icon=True
%}

Status options: completed, pending, failed, locked, not_started
Sizes: sm, md, lg
verification_type: "nin" or "bvn" — prepends "NIN " or "BVN " for clarity
-->

{% if status == "completed" %}
<span class="inline-flex items-center 
             {% if size == 'sm' %}px-2 py-0.5 text-xs
             {% elif size == 'lg' %}px-4 py-2 text-base

        <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M10 18a8 8 0 100-16 8 8 0 000 16zm3.707-9.293a1 1 0 00-1.414-1.414L9 10.586 7.707 9.293a1 1 0 00-1.414 1.414l2 2a1 1 0 001.414 0l4-4z" clip-rule="evenodd"/>
    </svg>
    {% endif %}
    {% if verification_type == "nin" %}NIN {% elif verification_type == "bvn" %}BVN {% endif %}{{ label|default:"Verified" }}
</span>

{% elif status == "pending" %}
<span class="inline-flex items-center 
             {% if size == 'sm' %}px-2 py-0.5 text-xs
             {% elif size == 'lg' %}px-4 py-2 text-base
             {% else %}px-3 py-1 text-sm{% endif %}
             font-semibold rounded-full bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800 border border-yellow-200">

    </svg>
    {% endif %}
    {% if verification_type == "nin" %}NIN {% elif verification_type == "bvn" %}BVN {% endif %}{{ label|default:"Pending" }}

{% block title %}Verification Update - KasuMarketplace{% endblock %}

    </p>

<p>Hello {{ vendor.user.first_name|default:"Vendor" }},</p>

<p>
    Thank you for your interest in selling on KasuMarketplace. After reviewing your 
    vendor application, we've identified some issues that need to be addressed before 
    we can approve your account.
</p>

<h2>Reason for Review</h2>
<div style="background-color: #fef3c7; padding: 20px; border-radius: 8px; border-left: 4px solid #f59e0b; margin: 20px 0;">
    <p style="margin: 0; color: #78350f; font-size: 15px; line-height: 1.6;">
        {{ rejection_reason|default:"We need additional information to complete your verification. Please review the requirements and resubmit your application." }}
    </p>
</div>

<h2>Common Issues</h2>
<p>Here are some common reasons verification may be delayed:</p>

<ul style="padding-left: 20px; color: #374151; line-height: 1.8;">
    <li><strong>Incomplete Information</strong> - Missing or incorrect NIN/BVN details</li>
    <li><strong>Document Quality</strong> - Blurry or unreadable ID documents</li>
    <li><strong>Mismatched Information</strong> - Names don't match across documents</li>
    <li><strong>Store Details</strong> - Insufficient or inappropriate store description</li>
    <li><strong>Category Issues</strong> - Selected category doesn't match products</li>
</ul>

<h2>What You Need to Do</h2>
<div style="margin: 25px 0;">
    <div style="padding: 15px; background-color: #eff6ff; margin-bottom: 12px; border-radius: 8px;">
        <p style="margin: 0; font-weight: bold; color: #1e40af;">1. Review the Feedback</p>
        <p style="margin: 5px 0 0 0; font-size: 14px; color: #1f2937;">
            Check the specific issues mentioned in the rejection reason above
        </p>
    </div>
    <div style="padding: 15px; background-color: #eff6ff; margin-bottom: 12px; border-radius: 8px;">
        <p style="margin: 0; font-weight: bold; color: #1e40af;">2. Update Your Information</p>
        <p style="margin: 5px 0 0 0; font-size: 14px; color: #1f2937;">
            Correct any errors or provide additional required documents
        </p>
    </div>
    <div style="padding: 15px; background-color: #eff6ff; border-radius: 8px;">
        <p style="margin: 0; font-weight: bold; color: #1e40af;">3. Resubmit for Review</p>
        <p style="margin: 5px 0 0 0; font-size: 14px; color: #1f2937;">
            Once corrected, we'll review your application again within 24 hours
        </p>
    </div>
</div>

<div style="text-align: center;">
    <a href="{{ base_url }}/vendors/verification/" class="button">
        Update Verification Details →
    </a>
</div>

<div class="info-box">
    <p style="margin: 0; font-size: 14px;">
        <strong>Need Help?</strong><br>
        If you're unsure about what needs to be corrected, our support team is ready to assist you. 
        Contact us at <a href="mailto:support@kasumarketplace.com" style="color: #3b82f6;">support@kasumarketplace.com</a>
    </p>
</div>

<div class="divider"></div>

<h2>Important Notes</h2>
<p style="font-size: 14px; color: #6b7280;">
    • You can resubmit your verification as many times as needed<br>
    • Make sure all information matches your official documents exactly<br>

{% extends 'vendors/base.html' %}

{% block title %}Add Product - KasuMarketplace{% endblock %}
{% block page_title %}Add New Product{% endblock %}
{% load static %}

{% block content %}
{% with vendor=request.user.vendorprofile %}

<div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto">

    <!-- Back Button -->
    <a href="{% url 'vendors:products_list' %}"
       class="inline-flex items-center text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 hover:text-primary mb-6 transition-colors">
        <svg class="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7"/>
        </svg>
        Back to Products
    </a>

    <!-- Form Card -->
    <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-200 p-4 sm:p-8">

        <form method="post"
              enctype="multipart/form-data"
              x-data="{
    submitting: false,
    subcategory: '{{ form.subcategory.value|default:'' }}',
    attributes: [],
    imageCount: 0,
    trackInventory: true,

    init() {
        if (this.subcategory) {
            this.loadAttributes(this.subcategory);
        }
        this.updateImageCount();
    },

    updateImageCount() {
        const inputs = document.querySelectorAll('input[type=file][name*=image]');
        this.imageCount = Array.from(inputs).filter(input => input.files && input.files.length > 0).length;
    },

    async loadAttributes(subcategoryId) {
        if (!subcategoryId) {
            this.attributes = [];
            return;
        }
        try {
            const response = await fetch(`/vendors/ajax/attributes/?subcategory_id=${subcategoryId}`);
            const data = await response.json();

            this.attributes = (data.attributes || []).map(attr => {
                const inputEl = document.querySelector(`[name='attr_${attr.id}']`);
                const submittedValue = inputEl ? inputEl.value : '';

                return {
                    ...attr,
                    value: submittedValue || ''
                };
            });
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error loading attributes:', error);
        }
    }
}"
              @submit="submitting = true">
            {% csrf_token %}

            {# Generic backend-error banner — only shown if the view passes general_error #}
            {% if general_error %}
            <div class="mb-6 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
                <p class="text-sm text-red-700">Something went wrong while saving. Please try again.</p>
            </div>
            {% endif %}

            {# Non-field form errors only — per-field errors render inline below #}
            {% if form.non_field_errors %}
            <div class="mb-6 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4">
                {% for error in form.non_field_errors %}
                <p class="text-sm text-red-700">{{ error }}</p>
                {% endfor %}
            </div>
            {% endif %}

            <!-- Main Category (locked) -->
            <div class="mb-6">
                <label class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-2">
                    Main Category
                    <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 ml-2 text-xs font-normal text-gray-500">
                        <svg class="size-3.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 11c1.66 0 3-1.34 3-3V6a3 3 0 10-6 0v2c0 1.66 1.34 3 3 3zm6 2H6a1 1 0 00-1 1v6a1 1 0 001 1h12a1 1 0 001-1v-6a1 1 0 00-1-1z"/>
                        </svg>
                        Locked
                    </span>
                </label>
                <div class="px-4 py-3 bg-gray-100 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg">
                    <p class="font-semibold text-gray-700">{{ request.user.vendorprofile.store.main_category.name }}</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Subcategory -->
            <div class="mb-6">
                <label for="{{ form.subcategory.id_for_label }}" class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-2">
                    Subcategory <span class="text-red-500">*</span>
                </label>
                <select name="{{ form.subcategory.html_name }}"
                        id="{{ form.subcategory.id_for_label }}"
                        x-model="subcategory"
                        @change="loadAttributes($event.target.value)"
                        class="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-primary"
                        required>
                    <option value="">Select Subcategory</option>

                <h2 class="text-base font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3">Specifications</h2>
                <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <template x-for="attr in attributes" :key="attr.id">
                        <div>
                            <label :for="'attr_' + attr.id"
                                   class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-2">
                                <span x-text="attr.name"></span>
                                <span class="text-red-500" x-show="attr.is_required"> *</span>
                            </label>

                                <input type="text"
                                       x-model="attr.value"
                                       :name="'attr_' + attr.id"

                                        <option :value="option" x-text="option"></option>
                                    </template>
                                </select>
                            </template>

                            <template x-if="attr.field_type === 'textarea'">
                                <textarea x-model="attr.value"

                                          :id="'attr_' + attr.id"
                                          :placeholder="attr.placeholder"
                                          :required="attr.is_required"
                                          rows="3"
                                          class="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-soft-blue focus:border-soft-blue"></textarea>
                            </template>

                            <template x-if="attr.field_type === 'checkbox'">

                                    <input type="checkbox"
                                           x-model="attr.value"
                                           :name="'attr_' + attr.id"
                                           :id="'attr_' + attr.id"
                                           value="true"
                                           class="w-4 h-4 text-soft-blue border-gray-300 rounded focus:ring-soft-blue">
                                    <span class="ml-2 text-sm text-gray-700" x-text="attr.help_text || 'Yes'"></span>
                                </label>

                            id="confirm-delete-button"

                        Delete

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
        const checkbox = document.getElementById('delete-confirmation');
        const button = document.getElementById('confirm-delete-button');
        const form = document.getElementById('delete-product-form');

        if (!checkbox || !button || !form) {
            return;
        }

        const toggleButton = () => {
            button.disabled = !checkbox.checked;
        };

        checkbox.addEventListener('change', toggleButton);
        toggleButton();

        form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {
            if (!checkbox.checked) {
                event.preventDefault();
                return;
            }
            button.disabled = true;
            button.textContent = 'Deleting...';
        });
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

{% extends 'vendors/base.html' %}

{% block title %}Edit Product - KasuMarketplace{% endblock %}
{% block page_title %}Edit Product{% endblock %}

{% block content %}

<div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto">

    <!-- Back Button -->
    <a href="{% url 'vendors:products_list' %}"
       class="inline-flex items-center text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 hover:text-primary mb-6 transition-colors">
        <svg class="w-4 h-4 mr-2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M15 19l-7-7 7-7"/>
        </svg>
        Back to Products
    </a>

    <!-- Form Card -->
    <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-200 p-4 sm:p-8">

        <form method="post"
              enctype="multipart/form-data"
              x-data="{
    submitting: false,
    subcategory: '{{ form.subcategory.value|default:product.subcategory.id }}',
    trackInventory: {{ product.track_inventory|yesno:'true,false' }},
    attributes: [],
    savedAttributes: {{ product.attributes|default:'{}'|safe }},
    existingImageCount: {{ product.images.count|default:0 }},
    newImageCount: 0,

    get totalImageCount() {
        return this.existingImageCount + this.newImageCount;
    },

    init() {
        this.loadAttributes(this.subcategory);
    },

    async loadAttributes(subcategoryId) {
        if (!subcategoryId) {
            this.attributes = [];
            return;
        }
        try {
            const response = await fetch(`/vendors/ajax/attributes/?subcategory_id=${subcategoryId}`);
            const data = await response.json();
            this.attributes = (data.attributes || []).map(attr => {
                const savedValue = this.savedAttributes[attr.id] || this.savedAttributes[String(attr.id)] || '';
                return { ...attr, value: savedValue };
            });
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error loading attributes:', error);
        }
    },

    updateNewImageCount() {
        const inputs = document.querySelectorAll('input[type=file][name*=image]');
        this.newImageCount = Array.from(inputs).filter(i => i.files && i.files.length > 0).length;
    }

<!-- Scripts -->
<script>
    // Character counter for description
    const descTextarea = document.querySelector('#{{ form.description.id_for_label }}');
    const descCount = document.getElementById('descCount');
    
    if (descTextarea && descCount) {
        descTextarea.addEventListener('input', function() {
            descCount.textContent = this.value.length;
        });
    }
    
    // Image preview functionality - moved outside and fixed
    function setupImagePreview(fileInputId, previewImageId) {
        const fileInput = document.getElementById(fileInputId);
        const previewImg = document.getElementById(previewImageId);
        
        if (!fileInput || !previewImg) return;
        
        fileInput.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
            const file = e.target.files[0];
            if (file && file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = function(event) {
                    previewImg.src = event.target.result;
                    // Remove placeholder styling for uploaded images
                    previewImg.style.padding = '0';
                    previewImg.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent';
                    previewImg.style.color = 'inherit';
                    previewImg.classList.remove('bg-gray-100');
                };
                reader.readAsDataURL(file);
            }
        });
    }
    
    // Setup preview for banner and logo
    setupImagePreview('id_banner', 'bannerPreview');
    setupImagePreview('id_logo', 'logoPreview');
    
    // Drag and drop for file uploads - fixed to use correct selectors
    const uploadBoxes = document.querySelectorAll('.upload-box-preview, .upload-box-preview-logo');
    
    uploadBoxes.forEach(box => {
        // Find the file input - check both inside the box and in parent container
        let input = box.querySelector('input[type="file"]');
        if (!input) {
            // If not found inside, check parent container
            const parent = box.closest('.relative');
            if (parent) {
                input = parent.querySelector('input[type="file"]');
            }
        }
        
        if (!input) return;
        
        // Prevent default drag behaviors
        ['dragenter', 'dragover', 'dragleave', 'drop'].forEach(eventName => {
            box.addEventListener(eventName, preventDefaults, false);
            document.body.addEventListener(eventName, preventDefaults, false);
        });
        
        function preventDefaults(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
        }
        
        // Highlight drop area when dragging over
        ['dragenter', 'dragover'].forEach(eventName => {
            box.addEventListener(eventName, highlight, false);
        });
        
        ['dragleave', 'drop'].forEach(eventName => {
            box.addEventListener(eventName, unhighlight, false);
        });
        
        function highlight(e) {
            box.style.borderColor = '#3B82F6';
            box.style.backgroundColor = 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.05)';
            box.style.borderStyle = 'dashed';
        }
        
        function unhighlight(e) {
            // Reset to original styling
            const img = box.querySelector('img');
            if (img) {
                box.style.borderColor = '';
                box.style.backgroundColor = '';
                box.style.borderStyle = '';
            }
        }
        
        // Handle dropped files
        box.addEventListener('drop', handleDrop, false);
        
        function handleDrop(e) {
            const dt = e.dataTransfer;
            const files = dt.files;
            
            if (files.length > 0) {
                const file = files[0];
                if (file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
                    // Set the file to the input
                    const dataTransfer = new DataTransfer();
                    dataTransfer.items.add(file);
                    input.files = dataTransfer.files;
                    
                    // Trigger change event to update preview
                    const changeEvent = new Event('change', { bubbles: true });
                    input.dispatchEvent(changeEvent);
                }
            }
        }
    });
    
    // Unsaved changes warning
    let formChanged = false;
    const form = document.getElementById('settingsForm');
    
    if (form) {
        form.addEventListener('change', function() {
            formChanged = true;
        });
        
        form.addEventListener('submit', function() {
            formChanged = false;
        });
        
        window.addEventListener('beforeunload', function(e) {
            if (formChanged) {
                e.preventDefault();
                e.returnValue = '';
                return '';
            }
        });
    }
</script>

{% endblock %}

<!-- MediaPipe Tasks Vision (ES module → exposed as window global for UMD widget) -->
<script type="module">
    import * as visionTasks from "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@mediapipe/tasks-vision@0.10.14/+esm";
    window.FaceLandmarksTasksVision = visionTasks;
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event('mediapipe:ready'));

<!-- LiveCapture widget -->
<script src="{% static 'vendors/js/live_capture.js' %}" defer></script>

<script>
async function loadBanksForBvn() {
    const select = document.querySelector('select[name="bank_name"]');
    if (!select) return;
    try {
        const res = await fetch('{% url "vendors:api_get_banks" %}');
        const data = await res.json();
        select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Select your bank --</option>';
        data.banks.forEach(bank => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = bank.name;
            opt.dataset.code = bank.code;
            opt.textContent = bank.name;
            select.appendChild(opt);
        });
    } catch (e) {
        select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Failed to load banks — refresh</option>';
    }
}
loadBanksForBvn();

function bvnVerificationForm() {
    return {
        bvnNumber: '',
        // 'idle' until widget auto-captures, then 'captured'
        // Widget manages its own internal loading/live/counting phases.
        cameraState: 'idle',
        capturedImage: '',
        submitting: false,

        init() {
            // LiveCapture widget fires this event when auto-capture succeeds.
            // This is the only integration point — no startCamera / captureSelfie
            // methods needed since the widget owns the full camera lifecycle.
            window.addEventListener('livecapture:captured', (e) => {
                this.capturedImage = e.detail.dataUrl;
                this.cameraState = 'captured';
            });
        },

        retake() {
            this.capturedImage = '';
            this.cameraState = 'idle';
            // Re-mount a fresh widget instance so camera restarts cleanly.
            this.$nextTick(() => {
                const root = document.getElementById('live-capture-root');
                if (root) {
                    root.innerHTML = '';
                    window.mountLiveCapture && window.mountLiveCapture('live-capture-root');
                }
            });
        },

        handleSubmit(e) {
            if (this.cameraState !== 'captured') {
                e.preventDefault();
                return;
            }
            this.submitting = true;
        }
    };
}
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
// ==========================================
// STORE LOCATION PICKER
// ==========================================

function captureLocation() {
    if (!navigator.geolocation) {
        showLocationStatus('error', 'Geolocation is not supported by your browser.');
        return;
    }

    setLocating(true);

    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
        function(position) {
            const lat = position.coords.latitude.toFixed(6);
            const lon = position.coords.longitude.toFixed(6);

            // Fill hidden form fields
            const latEl = document.getElementById('id_latitude');
            const lonEl = document.getElementById('id_longitude');
            if (latEl) latEl.value = lat;
            if (lonEl) lonEl.value = lon;

            // Show map preview with coords
            const mp = document.getElementById('map-preview');
            const coords = document.getElementById('map-coords');
            if (mp) mp.classList.remove('hidden');
            if (coords) coords.textContent = `${lat}, ${lon}`;

            // Reverse geocode using OpenStreetMap Nominatim (free, no API key)
            reverseGeocode(lat, lon);

            showLocationStatus('success', `✅ Location captured: ${lat}, ${lon}`);
            setLocating(false);
        },
        function(error) {
            setLocating(false);
            let msg = 'Could not get your location.';
            if (error.code === 1) msg = 'Location access denied. Please allow location in your browser.';
            if (error.code === 2) msg = 'Location unavailable. Try again or enter address manually.';
            if (error.code === 3) msg = 'Location request timed out. Try again.';
            showLocationStatus('error', msg);
        },
        {
            enableHighAccuracy: true,
            timeout: 10000,
            maximumAge: 0
        }
    );
}

function reverseGeocode(lat, lon) {
    fetch(`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/reverse?format=json&lat=${lat}&lon=${lon}`, {
        headers: { 'Accept-Language': 'en' }
    })
    .then(r => r.json())
    .then(data => {
        if (data && data.display_name) {
            const addressField = document.getElementById('id_address_display');
            // Only fill if empty
            if (addressField && !addressField.value.trim()) {
                const parts = data.address || {};
                const short = [
                    parts.road || parts.pedestrian || parts.suburb,
                    parts.city || parts.town || parts.village || parts.county,
                    parts.state
                ].filter(Boolean).join(', ');

                addressField.value = short || data.display_name;
            }
        }
    })
    .catch(() => {
        // Reverse geocode failed — no problem, user can type address
    });
}

function setLocating(loading) {
    const btn = document.getElementById('locate-btn');
    const icon = document.getElementById('locate-icon');
    const spinner = document.getElementById('locate-spinner');
    const label = document.getElementById('locate-label');

    if (loading) {
        if (btn) btn.disabled = true;
        if (icon) icon.classList.add('hidden');
        if (spinner) spinner.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (label) label.textContent = 'Getting location...';
    } else {
        if (btn) btn.disabled = false;
        if (icon) icon.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (spinner) spinner.classList.add('hidden');
        if (label) label.textContent = 'Update Location';
    }
}

function showLocationStatus(type, message) {
    const el = document.getElementById('location-status');
    if (!el) return;
    el.classList.remove('hidden', 'bg-green-50', 'text-green-700', 'border', 'border-green-200',
                        'bg-red-50', 'text-red-700', 'border-red-200');
    if (type === 'success') {
        el.className = 'mt-3 flex items-start gap-2 text-sm rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200';
    } else {
        el.className = 'mt-3 flex items-start gap-2 text-sm rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200';
    }
    el.textContent = message;
    el.classList.remove('hidden');
}

// On page load — if lat/lng already saved, show map preview
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const latEl = document.getElementById('id_latitude');
    const lonEl = document.getElementById('id_longitude');
    const lat = latEl ? latEl.value : '';
    const lon = lonEl ? lonEl.value : '';
    if (lat && lon) {
        const mp = document.getElementById('map-preview');
        const coords = document.getElementById('map-coords');
        if (mp) mp.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (coords) coords.textContent = `${lat}, ${lon}`;
        const label = document.getElementById('locate-label');
        if (label) label.textContent = 'Update Location';
        showLocationStatus('success', `✅ Location saved: ${lat}, ${lon}`);
    }
});
</script>

{% endblock %}

                            <select name="bank_name" id="bank_select"
                                            class="w-full px-4 py-3 border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-primary"
                                            required onchange="updateBankCode(this)">
                                <option value="">Loading banks...</option>
                            </select>
                            <input type="hidden" name="bank_code" id="bank_code_input" value="{{ wallet.bank_code }}">
                        </div>
                        <script>
                        async function loadBanks() {
                            const select = document.getElementById('bank_select');
                            const savedBank = '{{ wallet.bank_name|escapejs }}';
                            const savedCode = '{{ wallet.bank_code|escapejs }}';
  
                            try {
                                const res = await fetch('{% url "vendors:api_get_banks" %}');
                                const data = await res.json();
    
                                select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Select Bank --</option>';
                                data.banks.forEach(bank => {
                                    const opt = document.createElement('option');
                                    opt.value = bank.name;
                                    opt.dataset.code = bank.code;
                                    opt.textContent = bank.name;
                                    if (bank.name === savedBank || bank.code === savedCode) {
                                        opt.selected = true;
                                        document.getElementById('bank_code_input').value = bank.code;
                                    }
                                    select.appendChild(opt);
                                });
                            } catch(e) {
                                select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Failed to load banks — refresh page</option>';
                            }
                        }

                        function updateBankCode(select) {
                            const selected = select.options[select.selectedIndex];
                            document.getElementById('bank_code_input').value = selected.dataset.code || '';
                        }

                        loadBanks();
                </select>
                {% if messages %}
                    {% for message in messages %}
                        {% if 'bank' in message|lower or 'error' in message.tags %}
                        <p class="mt-2 text-sm text-red-600">{{ message }}</p>
                        {% endif %}
                    {% endfor %}
                {% endif %}
            </div>

            <div class="mb-6">
                <label for="id_account_number" class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-2">
                    Account Number
                    <span class="text-red-500">*</span>
                </label>

                       name="account_number"

                       maxlength="10"

                       required
                       pattern="[0-9]{10}"
                       oninput="this.value = this.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 10)">
                {% if messages %}

                    <td class="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <span class="inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold
                                     {% if transaction.transaction_type == 'AVAILABLE_CREDIT' or transaction.transaction_type == 'PENDING_CREDIT' %}bg-green-100 text-green-800
                                     {% elif transaction.transaction_type == 'REVERSAL' or transaction.transaction_type == 'WITHDRAWAL' %}bg-red-100 text-red-800
                                     {% else %}bg-gray-100 text-gray-800{% endif %}">
                            {% if transaction.transaction_type == 'AVAILABLE_CREDIT' or transaction.transaction_type == 'PENDING_CREDIT' %}
                            <svg class="w-3 h-3 mr-1" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
                                <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M10 18a8 8 0 100-16 8 8 0 000 16zm1-11a1 1 0 10-2 0v3.586L7.707 9.293a1 1 0 00-1.414 1.414l3 3a1 1 0 001.414 0l3-3a1 1 0 00-1.414-1.414L11 10.586V7z" clip-rule="evenodd"/>
                            </svg>
                            {% elif transaction.transaction_type == 'REVERSAL' or transaction.transaction_type == 'WITHDRAWAL' %}
                            <svg class="w-3 h-3 mr-1" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20">
                                <path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M10 18a8 8 0 100-16 8 8 0 000 16zm1-11a1 1 0 10-2 0v3.586L7.707 9.293a1 1 0 00-1.414 1.414l3 3a1 1 0 001.414 0l3-3a1 1 0 00-1.414-1.414L11 10.586V7z" clip-rule="evenodd"/>
                            </svg>
                            {% endif %}
                            {{ transaction.get_transaction_type_display }}
                        </span>
                    </td>
                    <td class="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-right">
                        <p class="text-lg font-bold 
                                  {% if transaction.transaction_type == 'AVAILABLE_CREDIT' or transaction.transaction_type == 'PENDING_CREDIT' %}text-green-600
                                  {% elif transaction.transaction_type == 'REVERSAL' or transaction.transaction_type == 'WITHDRAWAL' %}text-red-600
                                  {% else %}text-gray-900{% endif %}">
                            {% if transaction.transaction_type == 'AVAILABLE_CREDIT' or transaction.transaction_type == 'PENDING_CREDIT' %}+{% elif transaction.transaction_type == 'REVERSAL' or transaction.transaction_type == 'WITHDRAWAL' %}-{% endif %}₦{{ transaction.amount|floatformat:2 }}
                        </p>
                    </td>
                    <td class="px-6 py-4 whitespace-nowrap text-center">
                        {% if transaction.status == 'completed' %}
                        {% include 'vendors/components/verification_badge.html' with status="completed" label="Completed" size="sm" show_icon=True %}
                        {% elif transaction.status == 'pending' %}
                        {% include 'vendors/components/verification_badge.html' with status="pending" label="Pending" size="sm" show_icon=True %}
                        {% elif transaction.status == 'failed' %}
                        {% include 'vendors/components/verification_badge.html' with status="failed" label="Failed" size="sm" show_icon=True %}
                        {% endif %}
                    </td>
                </tr>
                {% endfor %}
            </tbody>
        </table>
    </div>
